The savings scare up about 50% off the regular price of an entrée, so it’s definitely worth a stop. Plus, it’s fun to see what other costumes are out there on the big night. <a class="more-link" href="https://www.milehighonthecheap.com/chipotle-boorito-halloween/#more-44799">[Read More…]</a></p>
